It provides that the term of office of the elected member of the Board shall expire before the end of his term of office if a judge is appointed to another judicial position, with the exception of the appointment of a regional court judge to serve as a district court judge, a military garrison court judge to serve as a military judge to the district court or a regional administrative court judge to serve as an NSA judge. As I understand it, the normal promotion of the current members of the council does not predict, immediately to the highest instance. Then maybe the court of appeal or the Supreme Court will also represent someone. Is this the future of this institution? Fear to think about how staff policy in the justice system will evolve over the next few years, because the main function of the council is creation function. 14 years ago, the Polish National Judicial Council was one of the founders of the European Judicial Council Network. This elite group now includes 24 councils from 20 countries. A few days ago we witnessed reports about the adoption of the Polish delegation at the annual meeting of the network of councils. The official declaration of the Lisbon network was adopted on 1 June 2018 and in it such words: presence at the general assembly of representatives of the Polish National Courts does not mean that the network recognises that its status within our association will be maintained when the Management Board completes its review of the position on this matter. In December 2017, the authorities of the European Judicial Council Network explicitly stated that the revised law on the National Courts of Justice does not fall within the standards of the Network and does not guarantee the proper functioning of a democratic system based on the rule of law. Soon we will host representatives of this organization in Poland, in which we are still part. This is no longer just a matter of a mere Irish judge, Madam Member. That is the term you recommended during the committee meeting to use... [[So what? Don't be so sensitive.]] ...as usual with an appealing and touching message to Members and Poles. All this in addition will be reflected in the future EU budget covering the years 2021 to 2027. It was not enough for the legislative and executive authorities to have their own representatives in this constitutional body. The revitalized line No 182 from Tarnowskie Góra to Zawiercie will cause trains from Katowice to the airport to take the route through Chorzów, Bytom and Tarnowskie Góra, and after reaching their destination they will continue their journey to Zawiercie. All of this had to change, therefore the Act adopted will allow not only to increase the economic potential of Poland, but also to increase the standard of living of the inhabitants of Poland, also of the district of Starogard. At the 62nd meeting of the Sejm, a law was adopted on the promotion of new investments. The existing new investment support mechanism has been linked to the need for public aid operators to obtain business permits on the so-called premises. Special economic zones. This caused not only a lot of difficulties, but also deepened the differences in economic development of many regions of Poland, which was also noticeable in the area of Pomeranian voivodship. Government estimates indicate that almost 13,000 could benefit from support. entrepreneurs throughout the country. It is worth adding that the location of the investment will be important. Above all, areas with the highest unemployment will be supported. The higher unemployment, the lower the value of investment will be required and more support will be provided. The law adopted introduces solutions to implement new mechanisms to support business decision-making throughout the country. It includes a number of mechanisms of tax preferences, special reliefs, which are designed to develop a favourable investment environment. This support will complement the existing resolution of the Special Economic Zones Act. The new regulations are an important instrument in the implementation of the Responsible Development Strategy. One of the elements of this strategy is to build tools that will really stimulate economic development and at the same time remove barriers to entrepreneurs. Our priority is to stimulate the growth of private investment, particularly in the areas of innovation development, technology development and exports, and to create stable jobs. This will not only create new, well-paid jobs, it will also be an opportunity for areas that have been forgotten by previous governments. I am thinking in particular of the areas of districts where effective programmes have not been presented for years to combat unemployment, to raise the salaries of workers. In combination with the lack of investment and industrial zones, this deepened the differences with regard to metropolitan cities or those closer to voivodship capitals. The Law on the promotion of new investments is also an update of the solutions used in special economic zones, the primary task of which was to stimulate economic growth in a specific territory of the country, e.g. by local application of tax relief or exemption. This formula is already gradually running out. New solutions respond to dynamically changing needs of entrepreneurs and investors, even if they reduce investment time. At the same time, it should be stressed that we will not give up special economic zones which will operate by 2026 under the rules. The coexistence of two laws will create a new system of investment incentives. New solutions contained in the bill will include investments in the form of construction of new production facilities and reinvestments. This is in line with the Responsible Development Strategy. The government project also envisages a complete departure from territorial restrictions on the possibility of receiving support in the form of tax preferences. The Law on the Promotion of New Investments implements a coherent investment infrastructure system and provides for legal solutions to improve the whole process, e.g. the possibility for the National Centre for Agricultural Assistance to transfer property intended for industrial and service purposes. I would like to add that as an entrepreneur, as a former entrepreneur, I see huge potential in the proposed solutions. They protect us and our possessions from fire, they're the first on the scenes of road accidents, they help in natural disasters. We remember the lovely pictures of firefighters saving the cat trapped in the tree, but it happens that they are also called by us to remove the nests of dangerous hornets or wasps. They're always on guard so that the rest of society can work safely, travel and rest. I also call on our fellow countrymen not to cause firemen to work by burning grass or by careless handling of fire near the forest. Let us remember that any fire can embrace vast areas, be difficult to control and end tragically. Let us not put our hands to their formation. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2018-06-07-sejm-63-3.u438	Dear Marshal! High Seym! A few days ago, on 5 June, it was the anniversary of the death of Bronisław Bohemia - an outstanding athlete, the most-sided Polish skier of the interwar period, three times an Olympian, a tatherman, a mountain rescuer. Bronisław Czech was a twenty-four-time Polish ski champion. He has repeatedly participated in the world ski championships. The best results were achieved in the Norwegian combination. In 1927 he took fifth place in this competition, and 2 years later - fourth. In the 1928 Olympic Games he took the 10th place in the Norwegian combination, four years later he was seventh. He was also a three-time record holder of Poland in the length of the jump. In 1928 he jumped 60.5 m and 62.5 m and in 1929 in Zakopane - 63 m. The fact that he was able to dance on roller skis, i.e. create such a sequence of evolution on skis that they seemed to actually dance. From 1932 he was a ski coach, he led a ski school on Kasprowy Wierch. In addition to mountain sports, Bronisław Bohemia also played athletics and table tennis. He was a versatile man. He was riding a motorcycle, swimming on canoes, playing violin and accordion. Besides, he painted on paper, carved in wood, wrote poems. Since he was 16 years old, he painted on glass, mostly flower motifs. He was an extremely modest man. He didn't talk about his climbing achievements in the Tatra Mountains, and it wasn't until years later that he was a great dancer. After the outbreak of World War II, Bronisław Bohemia joined the conspiracy activity, he was a Tatra courier. On May 14, when he was painting in his house, the Gestapo came for him and was arrested. A month later, on 14 June, he was in the first transport of prisoners to the Auschwitz concentration camp. In the camp he received camp number 349 and assigned to Block 4. Involved in conspiracy activities, participated in the resistance organized by rtm. Witold Pilecki. He could save his life, but he did not accept the proposal to train young German skiers. He stayed in the concentration camp. Until spring 1941, he worked in a carpenter shop and then in a wood spoon factory. In 1942, he was assigned to work at the camp museum. That's where he painted and carved. In the spring of 1944, his health deteriorated greatly. He was hospitalized in Oświęcim, where he died of exhaustion on June 5, 1944. Bronisław Czech was an outstanding athlete. He was also a great patriot. He did not cooperate with the Germans, chose to stay in Auschwitz and died there. On the 74th anniversary of the death of this outstanding Pole, it is worth mentioning his sports merits. Hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, hey, [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2018-06-07-sejm-63-3.u447	Thank you very much, sir.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-06-07-sejm-63-3.u448	Parliamentary Statement on the 70th anniversary of the death of Lt. Wenceslas Kitchen ps. Calm down. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Wacław Kuchnio was born on 23 March 1917 in Ryki, in the northern part of Małopolska Sandomierzna. He was from a large family of peasants. After graduating, he helped his parents on the farm. He served as a bomb squad in the years 1936-1938 as a bomb squad in 15. The infantry regiment in Dęblin. During the September campaign, he fought with his regiment in the army's composition. At the beginning of October 1939, after his defeat, he returned home to a military underground. He became a soldier of the Armed Forces Union of the National Riviera. In the summer of 1943, he completed a post-office course organized by the lieutenant. Mariana Bernaciaka, Orlika. Since the autumn of 1943, he has been an aide to Orlika. He was in charge of battle operations on several occasions on his own. In the summer of 1944, he fought with the German invader in the area of Opole Lublin. After the end of the fights with his unit he tried to get to Warsaw, but in the vicinity of Garwolin he was stopped by the Soviets. He made an effective attempt to escape, after which he returned to the Roar. He participated in many significant clashes, among others. in battles against CWB and UB: April 13, 1945 - the winning battle in Wola Zadybska, 24 May 1945 - the largest guerrilla battle in the Stocki Forest. He was once again a platoon commander, becoming an informal deputy to Orlik. After his death, he became the commandant for the diversion of Garwolin's circuit. In March 1947, he decided to end the fight and appeared with his soldiers. In April he left with his wife - Zofia Kuchnio, a former liaison and nurse of the branch of Orlika, in the vicinity of Wałbrzych and then to Podwarszawska Joy. He was under surveillance at all times. On June 6, 1948, he managed to escape from a raid in Joy, during which he shot an UB officer and wounded the other one badly. On June 8, 1948, he and his wife committed suicide. The bodies of the lieutenant and his spouses were exported by UB employees to an unknown location. In 2007, posthumously lieutenant. Wacław Kuchnio was awarded the Officer's Cross of the Order of the Rebirth of Poland. Thank you.
